<script>	
$(function(){
    var dw = $(document).width();
	$('.nav > ul > li').each(function(){
		$(this).hover(function(){
			$(this).find('.sub_nav').stop().slideDown();
			var lt = $(this).offset().left;
			var lw = $(this).outerWidth();
			var sw = $(this).find('.sub_nav').outerWidth();
			$(this).find('.sub_nav').css("left",'-'+((sw-lw)/2)+"px")
			
			if(lt+lw/2 < sw/2){
				$(this).find('.sub_nav').css("left",-lt+4+"px")
			}else if(lt+lw/2+sw/2 > dw){
				$(this).find('.sub_nav').css({"left":-(sw-(dw-lt))-4+"px"})
			}
		},function(){
			$(this).find('.sub_nav').stop().hide();
		});				   
	});
	$('.sub_nav').each(function(){
		if($(this).find('.sub_list>ul>li').size()>8)	
		{
		var html = '';
		$.each($(this).find('.sub_list>ul>li:gt(7)'),function(){
			html += $(this).prop("outerHTML");
			$(this).remove();
		})
		$(this).find('.sub_list>ul').after('<ul>' + html + '</ul>');
		}
		
	});
});
</script>

		},function(){       
			 $(".accont-dropdown").hide();
			 $('.login-box').removeClass('login-on');
		}); 
		
		$(window).scroll(function() {
			if( $(document).scrollTop() > 1 ){
				var scrollTop = $(document).scrollTop();
				$('#header-box').addClass("posFixed");
			}else{
				$('#header-box').removeClass("posFixed");
			}
			
		});
	})
</script>

<h3 class="tit">ABOUT US</h3>
<div class="container" ymcart_ve_tag="diy_about_us" ymcart_ve_type="textedit"><p>
<span style="color:#434343;"><span style="font-size:14px;"><span style="font-family:Arial;"><img align="left" alt="" height="360" src="//us01-imgcdn.dear-lover.com/43786/dl/fckeditfile/image/1541662194.jpg" width="425"/>Quanzhou Shiying Clothes Co., Ltd. is the owner of the famous Dear-Lover brand and a leading international manufacturer of <a href="/">wholesale women's clothing</a>. Located in Quanzhou City, one of Chinas most prominent industrial centers for clothing manufacturing, we boast of an annual output of over 3,000,000 pieces.<br/>
With over 8000 items and 10 different categories, you can find any women's clothing you need with us. We produce 5 new styles every day, and we have a warehouse capable of storing over 2,300,000 products, guaranteeing continuous stock. Everything is integrated with our website, our inventories are up to date, and we guarantee quick deliveries.<br/>
     <br/>
We offer three different kinds of services:</span></span> <span style="font-size:14px;"> </span></span><span style="font-size:14px;"> </span>
</p>
<div>
<span style="color:#434343;"><span style="font-family:Arial;"><strong>Wholesale</strong></span><br/>
<span style="font-family:Arial;">We work with more than 9000 small to medium wholesale and retail businesses around the world. Because of our advanced logistics, re-stocking your wholesale or retail clothing business is super easy with us. All the products available in our store are ready to despatch, we offer speedy deliveries, and we can work with low volume orders.<br/>
</span><strong style="font-family:Arial;">Dropshipping</strong></span>
</div>
<span style="color:#434343;"><span style="font-size:14px;"> </span> </span>
<p>
<span style="color:#434343;"><span style="font-size:14px;"> </span></span><span style="font-size:14px;"> </span>
</p>
<div>
<span style="color:#434343;"><span style="font-family:Arial;">Our global dropshipping business allows you to focus only on distributing our women’s clothing, while we take care of manufacturing, warehousing, re-stocking &amp; shipping for you. It’s never been easier to start selling right away.<br/>
</span><strong style="font-family:Arial;">OEM Service</strong></span>
</div>
<span style="color:#434343;"><span style="font-size:14px;"> </span> </span>
<p>
<span style="color:#434343;"><span style="font-size:14px;"><span style="font-family:Arial;">We can put your brand tag or label on our clothes since our clothes don't have our own tag or label. Do you have your own design that you need us to produce? Our quality control systems and high-end factory ensure that we can rapidly supply precisely the products and service you need.</span></span></span>

			$(".about-us").addClass("more_open")
		},function(){
			$(".about-us").removeClass("more_open")
		});	
		$(".why_btn").toggle(function(e) {
			$(".why").addClass("more_open")
		},function(){
			$(".why").removeClass("more_open")
		});	
		
	})

    //图片懒加载方法调用
    $(function() {
        $(".lazy").lazyload({
            effect: "fadeIn" // 载入使用何种效果
        });
    });

</script>

<script type="text/javascript">
    function submitNewsletter()
    {
        email = $("#newsletter_email").val();
        if (email == "")
        {
            $("#newsletter_email").tip("Please enter a valid email address.", 15, 0, 2000);
            return;
        }
        $isvalidemail = /^((([a-z]|\d|[!#\$%&'\*\+\-\/=\?\^_`{\|}~]|[\u00A0-\uD7FF\uF900-\uFDCF\uFDF0-\uFFEF])+(\.([a-z]|\d|[!#\$%&'\*\+\-\/=\?\^_`{\|}~]|[\u00A0-\uD7FF\uF900-\uFDCF\uFDF0-\uFFEF])+)*)|((\x22)((((\x20|\x09)*(\x0d\x0a))?(\x20|\x09)+)?(([\x01-\x08\x0b\x0c\x0e-\x1f\x7f]|\x21|[\x23-\x5b]|[\x5d-\x7e]|[\u00A0-\uD7FF\uF900-\uFDCF\uFDF0-\uFFEF])|(\\([\x01-\x09\x0b\x0c\x0d-\x7f]|[\u00A0-\uD7FF\uF900-\uFDCF\uFDF0-\uFFEF]))))*(((\x20|\x09)*(\x0d\x0a))?(\x20|\x09)+)?(\x22)))@((([a-z]|\d|[\u00A0-\uD7FF\uF900-\uFDCF\uFDF0-\uFFEF])|(([a-z]|\d|[\u00A0-\uD7FF\uF900-\uFDCF\uFDF0-\uFFEF])([a-z]|\d|-|\.|_|~|[\u00A0-\uD7FF\uF900-\uFDCF\uFDF0-\uFFEF])*([a-z]|\d|[\u00A0-\uD7FF\uF900-\uFDCF\uFDF0-\uFFEF])))\.)+(([a-z]|[\u00A0-\uD7FF\uF900-\uFDCF\uFDF0-\uFFEF])|(([a-z]|[\u00A0-\uD7FF\uF900-\uFDCF\uFDF0-\uFFEF])([a-z]|\d|-|\.|_|~|[\u00A0-\uD7FF\uF900-\uFDCF\uFDF0-\uFFEF])*([a-z]|[\u00A0-\uD7FF\uF900-\uFDCF\uFDF0-\uFFEF])))$/i.test(email);
        if (!$isvalidemail)
        {
            $("#newsletter_email").tip("Please enter a valid email address.", 15, 0, 2000);
            return;
        }
        $.ajax({
            url: '/h-module-submitNewsletter.html',
            type: 'POST',
            data: {
                email: email
            },
            dataType: 'text',
            cache: false,
            success: function(msg) {
                if ($.trim(msg) == "1") {
                    $("#newsletter_email").tip("Subscribe Succeed", 15, 0, 2000);
                } else {
                    $("#newsletter_email").tip("Subscribe Failed", 15, 0, 2000);
                }
            },
            error: function() {
                alert('Connection failed, please refresh');
            }
        });
    }
	//input value:Your Email
	$(window).on("load",function(){
		$("#newsletter_email").val("Your Email Address")
	});
	
	function newsletterfocus(){
		if($("#newsletter_email").val()=="Your Email Address"){
			
			$("#newsletter_email").val("")
			
		}
	}
	function newsletterblur(){
			
				if($("#newsletter_email").val()==""){
			
			$("#newsletter_email").val("Your Email Address")
			
		}
		
	}
</script>

<script>
    function getLocalTime(i) {
        //参数i为时区值数字，比如北京为东八区则输进8,西5输入-5
        if (typeof i !== 'number') return;
        var d = new Date();
        //得到1970年一月一日到现在的秒数
        var len = d.getTime();
        //本地时间与GMT时间的时间偏移差
        var offset = d.getTimezoneOffset() * 60000;
        //得到现在的格林尼治时间
        var utcTime = len + offset;
        return new Date(utcTime + 3600000 * i);
    }

    var nd = getLocalTime(8);
    YY = nd.getFullYear() + '-';
    MM = nd.getMonth() + 1 + '-';
    dd = nd.getDate() + ' ';
    hh = nd.getHours() + ':';
    mm = nd.getMinutes() + ':';
    ss = nd.getSeconds();
    console.log(YY + MM + dd + hh + mm + ss);
    //	console.log(YY + MM + dd + '08:00');
    //	console.log(YY + MM + dd + '18:30');
    //弹框
    var now_time = Date.parse(YY + MM + dd + hh + mm + ss);//当前时间
    var eight_oclock = Date.parse(YY + MM + dd + '08:00'); //8:00
    var half_past_six = Date.parse(YY + MM + dd + '18:30');//18:30
    var day = new Date(now_time);
    var today = new Array('星期日', '星期一', '星期二', '星期三', '星期四', '星期五', '星期六');
    var week = today[day.getDay()];
    // if(now_time>half_past_six || week=='星期六' || week=='星期日'){

<script language="javascript">
$(function() {
	$('.discount-timer').each(function() {
		$(this).genTimer({
			beginTime: "2019/10/23 19:25:12",
			targetTime: $(this).attr('endTime'),
			callback: function(time) {
				this.html(time);
			}
		});
		});
		
		$('.quickview').click(function(){
           	_this=$(this);
		   
		  	$('.mini_detail').addClass("show");
          	createAjaxLoading();
         	var pro_url = "/h-product-detail.html?goods_id="+_this.parents(".product_inner").find('.quickcart_box').attr('goods_id')+"&type=quick&height=750";
          	$('#mini_detail').append('<iframe id="mini_iframe" width="960" scrolling="no" height="750" frameborder="false" allowtransparency="false" style="border: medium none;" src='+pro_url+' name="main"></iframe>');
              removeAjaxLoading();
        });
        $('.close_icon').click(function(){
            $(this).parent().parent().removeClass("show");
			$(this).siblings().remove();
        });
        $('.mini_detail_bg').click(function(){
            $(this).parent().removeClass("show");
			$(this).parent().find('iframe').remove();
        });
		
  var goods_ids = new Array();
	
	$('.tlike').each(function(){
   	goods_ids[goods_ids.length] = $(this).attr('data_id');
	});
	$.ajax({
		url: "/h-user-wishListStatus.html?goods_ids=" + goods_ids.join(','),
		cache: false,
		dataType : "json",
		success: function(data){
			var data = data.data;
			$(".tlike").each(function(){
				var td =$(this).attr('data_id');
				if(data[$(this).attr('data_id')]=='1'){
					$(this).children("i").removeClass("icon-xin");
					$(this).children("i").addClass("icon-xin1 cur");
					
				}
			});
		}
	}); 
		 
	$('.quickaddtocart').click(function(){

             $(this).parents('.product_inner').find('.quickcart_box').show();

             _this=$(this);

             if($(this).parents('.product_inner').find('.quickcart_box').html()=="" || $(this).parents('.product_inner').find('.quickcart_box').html()=="Loading...")

             {

                 createAjaxLoading();

                 $(this).parents('.product_inner').find('.quickcart_box').css('position','absolute');

                 $(this).parents('.product_inner').find('.quickcart_box').load("/h-cart-quickcart.html?goods_id="+$(this).parents('.product_inner').find('.quickcart_box').attr('goods_id'),'',function(){                     _this.parent().find('.quickcart_box').css('position','');

                     removeAjaxLoading();

                 });

             }

         }

     );






	$('.tlike').click(function(){
            var _this = $(this);
            var _val  = parseInt(_this.find('font').text());
            var goods_id = _this.attr('data_id');
            $.ajax({
                 url :'/h-user-addFavorites.html',
                type:'POST',
                data:{
                    goods_id : goods_id
                },
                dataType: 'json',
                cache: false,
                success: function(res) {
					
                    if(res == 1){
                        $(_this).tip("Add to favorite succeed.", 15, 0, 2000);
                        _this.find('font').text(_val+1);
						$(_this).find('span').addClass("icon-xin1 cur");
						
                    }else if(res== 2){
                        $(_this).tip("Please Login first", 15, 0, 2000);
                    }else if(res== 3){
                        $(_this).tip("Already added", 15, 0, 2000);
                        $(_this).find('span').addClass("icon-xin1 cur");
                    }else{
                        $(_this).tip("Add to favorite failed.", 15, 0, 2000);
                    }
                },
                error:function(){
                    alert('Connection failed, please refresh');
                },
                beforeSend: function(){
                    createAjaxLoading();
                },
                complete: function(){
                    removeAjaxLoading();
                }
            });
			
			
        });
	

		$('.home-discount-timer').each(function() {
		$(this).genTimer({
			beginTime: "2019/10/23 19:25:12",
			targetTime: $(this).attr('endTime'),
			callback: function(time) {
				
				var time = time
				var a = time.substring(time.indexOf("seconds")+9,time.length-7);
				
				this.html('<span class="hours">'+'<span class="value">'+a.split(":")[0]+'</span><b>Hours</b>'+'</span><span class="d">:</span>'+'<span class="minutes">'+'<span class="value">'+a.split(":")[1]+'</span><b>Mins</b>'+'</span><span class="d">:</span>'+'<span class="seconds">'+'<span class="value">'+a.split(":")[2]+'</span><b>Secs</b>'+'</span>');
			}
		});
	});
  
  
  });

//图片懒加载方法调用
$(function() {
    $(".lazy").lazyload({
        effect: "fadeIn" // 载入使用何种效果
    });

    $("img.lazy").closest('a').mouseover(function(){
        var first_pic = $(this).find('img.lazy').attr("src");
        var second_image = $(this).find('img.lazy').attr("second_image");
        $(this).find('img.lazy').attr("src",second_image)
    });

    $("img.lazy").closest('a').mouseout(function(){
        var first_pic = $(this).find('img.lazy').attr("data-original");
        var second_image = $(this).find('img.lazy').attr("second_image");
        $(this).find('img.lazy').attr("src",first_pic);
    })

});
